# Necklaces
Aliases: Necklace
Categories: Equipment, Necklaces
Necklaces are pieces of [[Equipment]] that the player can equip in the [[Equipment Menu]].

# Fame Ranks
Aliases: Fame, Fame Rank
Categories: Events, Mechanics
[Fame Ranks]
Type: [[Currency]]
Players can reach Fame Ranks by contributing to [[City Projects]] and spending [[Bits]] and [[SkyBlock Gems]] mainly through [[Elizabeth]] at the [[Community Center]].
## Earning Fame
Players can earn Fame in the following ways:
- 1 Fame per 1 [[Bits|Bit]] spent at the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]];
- 200 Fame per 1 [[SkyBlock Gems|SkyBlock Gem]] spent in the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]];
- 200 Fame for every contribution made to a [[City Projects|city project]];
  - Some city project contributions may provide additional Fame bonuses.
Fame is account-wide and transfers across profiles. The player's current progress can be viewed in the /boostercookie menu.

# Fishing Festival
Aliases: FF
Categories: Events, Fishing Festival, Mayor Election
[Fishing Festival]
Category: [[Events]]
Next [[Marina]]'s Festival: : Countdown:
Next [[Foxy]]'s Festival: : Countdown:
The Fishing Festival is an [[Events|Event]] that occurs when [[Marina|Mayor Marina]] is elected as Mayor with her Fishing Festival active, or when [[Foxy|Mayor Foxy]]'s Extra Event perk or [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s Perkpocalypse perk is rotated to a Fishing Festival.
During the Fishing Festival, players are able to fish up Sharks, special [[Sea Creatures]] that drop [[Shark Fin|Shark Fins]] and different varieties of Shark Teeth.
## Overview
### Timing
[[Marina]] schedules 12 Fishing Festivals throughout the year, occurring on the first 3 days of each month.
Regular Fishing Festivals Schedules:
|In-Game Date|Countdown|
|-|-|
|1st of Early Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Spring|: Countdown:|
Other factors affect the time a Fishing Festival takes place:
- If [[Foxy|Mayor Foxy]] is in office with her Extra Event perk active, a Bonus Fishing Festival occurs from 22nd of Late Summer with the same duration.
- A Bonus Fishing Festival from [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s Perkpocalypse occurs at exactly 18:00 on the same SkyBlock day of the beginning of a rotation to perks from Marina (or 18 hours in [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]] / 15 minutes in real-life time following the beginning of rotation to perks from Marina). The bonus event has the same duration as a normal Festival.
### Mechanics
During the Fishing Festival, Sharks have a base 15% chance to replace all Sea Creatures. The chances for Sharks to replace Sea Creatures can be increased with the following methods:
- +1-5% from [[Doug]]'s Shark Sonar perk
- +20% from [[Shark Bait]]
- +20% from [[Shark Scale Armor]]'s Festival Fisher tiered bonus
- +10.1-20% from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Megalodon Pet]]'s Feeding Frenzy perk
totalling to a +80% chance for Sharks to replace any Sea Creature.

|[[Nurse Shark]]|Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|2,500 [[Health]]|2–4 [[Shark Fin]] 0–1 [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] 0–1 [[Carnival Ticket]]|
|[[Blue Shark]]|Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]])|25,000 [[Health]]|4–8 [[Shark Fin]] 0–1 [[Blue Shark Tooth]] 0–1 [[Carnival Ticket]]|
|[[Tiger Shark]]|Fishing XVIII (18) ([[Fishing]])|250,000 [[Health]]|8–16 [[Shark Fin]] 0–1 [[Tiger Shark Tooth]] 0–1 [[Carnival Ticket]] 0–1x EPIC Megalodon Pet ([[Megalodon Pet]])|
|[[Great White Shark]]|Fishing XXIV (24) ([[Fishing]])|1,500,000 [[Health]]|16–32 [[Shark Fin]] 0–1 [[Great White Shark Tooth]] 0–1 [[Carnival Ticket]] 0–1x LEGENDARY Megalodon Pet ([[Megalodon Pet]])|

## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|60 Minutes before the event starts|FISHING FESTIVAL The event starts in 3 days!|
|40 Minutes before the event starts|FISHING FESTIVAL The event starts in 2 days!|
|20 Minutes before the event starts|FISHING FESTIVAL The event starts in 1 day!|
|Event Starts|FISHING FESTIVAL The festival is now underway! Break out your fishing rods and watch out for sharks!|
|Event Ends|FISHING FESTIVAL The festival has concluded! Time to dry off and repair your rods!|
## Trivia
- The Fishing Festival is marked with a Fishing Rod on the in-game [[Calendar]].
- It is possible to have both Fishing Festival mobs and [[Spooky Festival]] mobs appear while fishing during the Fishing Festival that occurs after the [[Spooky Festival]].

# Jacob's Farming Contest
Aliases: Farming Contest, JFC, Jacob's Contest, Jacob's Farming Contests, Jacobs Contest
Categories: Events, Farming
Jacob's Farming Contest
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The top players who increase their collection the most earn [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] and unique rewards!
— [[Jacob]]
Jacob's Farming Contest is an [[Events|event]] that takes place every 3 SkyBlock days, and lasts for 20 minutes (real life time), during which three random crops from the [[Collections|Farming Collection]] are selected for players to collect.
## Mechanics
### Timing
The event lasts for 1 day in [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]], or 20 minutes in real-life time. The interval of Jacob's Farming Contest is 3 days in [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]], or 1 hour in real-life time.
### Requirements
The player needs to be at least [[Farming|Farming X (10)]] to participate, and must talk to [[Jacob]] at least once. At least 100 of the current event crop must be collected to earn any rewards.
### Rewards
There are 5 brackets of rewards: BRONZE, SILVER, GOLD, PLATINUM and DIAMOND. Each bracket earns better rewards.
If [[Finnegan]] is elected as Mayor or Minister with the GOATed perk, the amount of players included in each bracket is increased by up to 10%.
|Bracket|Top % Required GOATed ([[Finnegan]]) Perk Active|Medals|Other Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|
|Bracket|✕ No ✔ Yes|Medals|Other Rewards|
||100 crops||1 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|BRONZE|60% 70%|1x BRONZE|10 [[Jacob's Ticket]] 1 [[Carnival Ticket]] 1x Turbo-Crop I ([[Turbo-Crop]]) 40 [[Bits]]|
|SILVER|30% 40%|1x SILVER|15 [[Jacob's Ticket]] 1 [[Carnival Ticket]] 1x Turbo-Crop I ([[Turbo-Crop]]) 50 [[Bits]]|
|GOLD|10% 20%|1x GOLD|25 [[Jacob's Ticket]] 2 [[Carnival Ticket]] 1x Turbo-Crop I ([[Turbo-Crop]]) 60 [[Bits]]|
|PLATINUM|5% 10%|1x GOLD 1x BRONZE|30 [[Jacob's Ticket]] 2 [[Carnival Ticket]] 1x Turbo-Crop I ([[Turbo-Crop]]) 70 [[Bits]]|
|DIAMOND|2% 5%|1x GOLD 1x SILVER|35 [[Jacob's Ticket]] 3 [[Carnival Ticket]] 1x Turbo-Crop I ([[Turbo-Crop]]) 80 [[Bits]]|
[[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] are items that can be spent with [[Anita]] to buy items to help build farms, as well as chips that grant [[Crop Fortune]] for the active crop during the contest and increased experience gain for [[Farming Tools]]. They are also used to buy [[Farming Fortune]] boosts and increase players' [[Farming]] level cap to 60 (LX).
Turbo-Crop books are [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] with enchantments that grant the player 5 of the corresponding [[Crop Fortune]] per level, up to 5. Level 4 and 5 Turbo-Crop enchantments require a having earned a BRONZE and SILVER in their crop, respectively, otherwise they act as if the player didn't have the Turbo-Crop enchantment at all. The player will always get a book for the crop whose contest they participated in. The books may also appear as a reward from the [[Librarian]] visitor on [[The Garden]].
The amount of [[Bits]] earned from contests is multiplied by the player's Bits Multiplier, based on their [[Fame Ranks|Fame Rank]].
Earning a GOLD medal allows the player to increase their [[Farming]] level cap by one per crop they earned a GOLD medal in, up to 60 (LX).
The PLATINUM and DIAMOND tiers do not have their own medals, but rather give the player additional medals and other rewards as seen in the table above. Reaching the PLATINUM or DIAMOND bracket in all crops will also reward the player with unique [[SkyBlock Levels|emblems]].
## Previous and Upcoming Events
Current and upcoming Jacob's Farming Contests can be viewed the tab list, Jacob's menu, as well as the SkyBlock [[Calendar]].
## Tips
- It is possible to kill [[Pests]] during contests, as well as prepare up to 20 Pests ahead of time (8 on the Garden and 12 in [[Vermin Trap|Vermin Traps]]) and kill them as soon as the contest starts in order to achieve much higher scores.
  - Furthermore, a player can also prepare all [[Greenhouse]] plots with the crop that will be in the contest. This allows the player to instantly get a high amount of crops depending on their [[The Garden|Greenhouse Upgrades]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Trivia
- Contest brackets each include one additional player on top of the normal percentage, so players may score in a higher bracket if they were the one closest to it.
- When starting a contest or warping within the last 3 seconds of a contest, [[Jacob]] may be unable to figure out the player's placing. However, the score will be calculated correctly regardless.
- The chat message may report the wrong bracket if it was very close and changed last second. [[Jacob]] will tell players the actual bracket they placed in when talking to him.
- If there is a Jacob's Contest ongoing, scheduled server restarts will be delayed until 20 seconds after it is over. However, unlike [[Dungeons]] and [[Kuudra]], game update restarts are not delayed.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 6, 2020|0.9.11|Added Jacob's Farming Contest.|
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Added PLATINUM and DIAMOND brackets.|
|Nov 21, 2023|[[Finnegan]] now only boosts the PLATINUM and DIAMOND brackets by 5% and 3%, respectively.|

# Mining Fiesta
Aliases: Fiesta, Mining Festival, Mining Fiest, Mining fiest, National Mining Month
Categories: Events, Mining Fiesta, Mayor Election
[Mining Fiesta]
Category: [[Events]]
The Mining Fiesta is an [[Events|Event]] that occurs when [[Cole|Mayor Cole]] is elected as Mayor with his Mining Fiesta perk active, perk or [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s Perkpocalypse perk is rotated to a Mining Fiesta.
During the Mining Fiesta, players mining on public islands can obtain [[Refined Mineral|Refined Minerals]] and [[Glossy Gemstone|Glossy Gemstones]].
## Overview
The event lasts the entirety of [[Cole]]'s mayoral term, provided his Mining Fiesta perk is active.
### Mechanics
During the Mining Fiesta, on Public Islands:
- +25 [[Mining Wisdom]];
- +25% [[Mining Fortune]];
- Ability to buy [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]] from [[Brynmor]]:
  - [[Fiesta Flask]] grants +25% [[Mining Fortune]] and +100% chance to finding a [[Refined Mineral]] and [[Glossy Gemstone]] for 1 hour during fiesta.
  - [[Attributes|Fiesta Fortune I-X]] ( [[Packrat Shard]]) grants 5-50 [[Gemstone Fortune]] during fiesta.
- Additionally, players can collect:
  - [[Refined Mineral|Refined Minerals]] from [[Ores]], [[Ores|Pure Ores]] and [[Dwarven Metals]]
  - [[Glossy Gemstone|Glossy Gemstones]] from [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
Their chances:
- scale with the [[Breaking Power]] of the block mined;
- can be increased through other means;
- are not penalized on blocks broken through [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] such as [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickobulus]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Sheer Force]].
Chance=Breaking Power5,000×(1+∑Multipliers)
  Where ∑Multipliers is the sum of the multipliers.

## Trivia
- The Mining Fiesta was previously called the National Mining Month and only took place during Late Summer and Early Autumn. This was changed to accommodate players living in multiple time zones, by splitting them into 2 events, then into 5: following this last change, one of them eventually became bugged since it was scheduled before the beginning of the election.

# New Year Celebration
Aliases: New Year Event, New year event
Categories: Events, New Year Celebration
New Year Celebration
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The New Year's Celebration is an [[Events|Event]] that takes place Late Winter, 29-31 (in-game time). During this time, the [[Baker]] appears next to [[Jerry]] in the [[Village]], allowing players to claim one [[New Year Cake]] for the year, or purchase a [[New Year Cake Bag]] for 250,000 [[Coins]]. This can hold up to 54 of your New Year Cakes and gives +1 [[Health]] for every unique Cake stored inside.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Day Before Event|The New Year's Celebration event is starting in 1 day!|
|Event Starts|A Event: New Year's Celebration! A Everyone is having a party in the Village!; Go there and talk to the Baker to get your New Year's Cake!|
|Event Starts ([[Stranded]] only (This content is only available on the Stranded game mode.))|Baker has appeared on your island!|
|Event Ends (when player in hub)|A Happy New Year to Everyone! A|
## Trivia
- If a person were to, starting with no previous cakes, collect a new cake every event without fail, they could fill the cake bag in 279 days (slightly under 40 weeks, about 76% of a year).
- Due to an error for the 1st year, you could switch lobbies and get multiple cakes, some players collected over 100 cakes this way.
- During the 69th New Year Celebration, SkyBlock went under maintenance for the v0.7.9 update, closing the game to 1[[Ranks|Default]] ranks and preventing them from obtaining the New Year Cake. This closure was lifted shortly after the maintenance announcement and placed again after the event ended.
- During the 71st New Year Celebration, players were given the 0th New Year Cake instead of the 71st New Year Cake. This was possibly a glitch from the 2020 anniversary.
- This is the only event that was not canceled by [[Dante]] when he was Mayor.
- During SkyBlock Year 144-146 (from June 23 to July 4, 2021), the [[Baking Specialist]] appeared at [[Coordinates|-12, 70, -44]] and handed out the Year 143 [[New Year Cake]], because Hypixel was in maintenance mode during the New Year Celebration of Year 143. During the New Year Celebration of Year 144 and 145, both the Baker and Baking Specialist appeared.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added New Year Celebration.|

# Season of Jerry
Aliases: Season Of The Jerry, Season of the Jerry
Categories: Events, Season of Jerry
Jerry's Workshop Events
Jerry's Workshop Opens
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Season of Jerry
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Season of Jerry is a Winter Solstice event in Hypixel SkyBlock, taking place on Day 24 of Late Winter every year.
The main event in the Season of Jerry is the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event that lasts 5 rounds, and in between each round there's a timer of 8 minutes in total. At the end of each round there will be presents depending on the amount of damage done.
## Accessing Jerry's Workshop
While the main event is only available for 1 hour, [[Jerry's Workshop]] can be accessed during the whole "Late Winter" season, which is around 8 hours before the event and 1 hour after. It can be accessed in the following ways:
- Clicking on [[Jerry]] on the [[Private Island]]
- Using the [[SkyBlock Menu]], an icon will appear above the [[Fast Travel]] button
- Clicking on [[Jerry]] in the [[Village]]
- Using commands /savethejerrys, /warp jerry, /warp workshop or /warp winter
## Defend Jerry's Workshop
While many different activities can be done during the Season of Jerry, the main event itself that is available for only 1 hour during the Late Winter season is defending Jerry's Workshop.
This requires fighting various lava-based enemies, with Magma Cubes being the biggest threat that destroys rewards. These magma cubes can only be damaged with snowballs, making it so that every player has a fair chance at winning the event.
## Trivia
- The Season of Jerry was the third [[Events|Event]] introduced to [[Hypixel]] [[SkyBlock]].
- During [[Dante|Dante's]] term as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], this [[Events|Event]], alongside the [[Spooky Festival]] and [[Traveling Zoo]] were canceled.
- The Season of Jerry is a great way to earn money, as the amount of [[Gifts]] received at the end of each round can be sold to the [[Bazaar]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Season of Jerry.|

# SkyBlock Anniversary
Aliases: 1st Anniversary, 2nd Anniversary, 3rd Anniversary, 4th Anniversary, 5th Anniversary, 6th Anniversary, 7th Anniversary, 8th Anniversary, 9th Anniversary, Anniversary, Party Time, Party Time!, SkyBlock Anniversaries
Categories: Events
Event Icon
  Not to be confused with [[New Year Celebration]], which celebrates a new SkyBlock year.
The SkyBlock Anniversary is a recurring [[Events|event]] that celebrates the real-life anniversary of when SkyBlock was released to Hypixel. During the event, an NPC named [[Simon]] gives out [[Hatcessories]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
## Events
### 1st SkyBlock Anniversary: Party Time!
Party Time! was the first SkyBlock Anniversary event. It started on June 10, 2020 at 11:00 PM Eastern Daylight Time and lasted for 24 hours.
- [[Simon]] handed [[Crab Hat of Celebration]] at the [[Event Stand]] (behind the [[Bank]]) at [[Coordinates|35, 71, -48]].
- The event was called 1st Party Time.
### 2nd SkyBlock Anniversary
The 2nd SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2021.
- There was no event or buffs.
### 3rd SkyBlock Anniversary
The 3rd SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2022. It started on June 10, 2022 at 6:35 PM EDT and lasted for 7 days (168 hours).
- [[Simon]] handed the [[Crab Hat of Celebration]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] to players.
- The [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] and [[Shiny Pig]] features were available to players.
- A temporary 15% buff to [[Skills|Skill]] Experience for all players.
### 4th SkyBlock Anniversary
The 4th SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2023. It started on June 12, 2023 at 6:15 PM EDT and lasted for 7 days (168 hours).
- [[Simon]] handed the [[Sloth Hat of Celebration]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] to players.
- The [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] and [[Shiny Pig]] features were available to players, including a replacement for the [[Dante's Talisman]].
- A temporary 20% buff to [[Skills|Skill]] Experience for all players.
Hypixel also released a video named "4 Years of SkyBlock", and it was made available on Sunday, June 11 at 18:00 (UTC).
### 5th SkyBlock Anniversary
The 5th SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2024. It started on June 11, 2024 at 11:55 AM EDT and lasted for 7 days (168 hours).
- [[Simon]] handed out the [[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] to players.
- The [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] and [[Shiny Pig]] features were available to players.
- A temporary 20% buff to [[Skills|Skill]] and [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Experience for all players.
### 6th SkyBlock Anniversary
The 6th SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2025, starting on June 11, 2025 at 11:55 AM EDT and lasted 7 days (168) hours.
- [[Simon]] handed out the [[6th Anniversary Balloon Hat]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] to players.
- A temporary 20% buff to [[Skills|Skill]] and [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Experience as well as +10 [[Magic Find]] for all players.
### 7th SkyBlock Anniversary
The 7th SkyBlock Anniversary occurred in year 2026, started on June 11, 2026 at 11:55 AM EDT and lasted 7 days (168) hours.
- [[Simon]] gave out [[7th Anniversary Cake Hat]] to players.
- A temporary 20% buff to [[Skills|Skill]] and [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Experience as well as 10 [[Magic Find]] for all players.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Player joins SkyBlock; during an Anniversary Prior to 7th Anniversary|A Happy Anniversary! A SkyBlock is celebrating its [n-th] birthday!! Talk to Simon in the Village to celebrate!|
|Player joins SkyBlock; during an Anniversary 7th Anniversary|Welcome to Hypixel Skyblock! 7TH ANNIVERSARY!; Earn +20% Dungeons and Skill EXP and +10✯ Magic Find!|
## Trivia
- In 2020, Party Time! was revealed in the [[Calendar]] 24 hours before it started.
- There was no event in 2021.
- For the 2022 event, [[Simon]] is situated next to the [[Colosseum]] at [[Coordinates|76, 69, -44]].
- The 2022 event was originally scheduled to end on June 12, but it was extended to June 17.
  - At the same time as this change, the event was brought to the [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] Gamemode.
- The first time that the SkyBlock Anniversary and Century Celebration event will overlap will be on June 12th 2077 of real life time, in the SkyBlock Year 4100, and it will theoretically happen again every 58 years.
- For the 2024 event, [[Simon]] was situated next to the [[Bazaar]] at [[Coordinates|-21, 69, -78]].
- The boost to Skill Experience from this event is applied as a multiplier after all other modifiers are calculated, including [[Champion]], [[Stats|Wisdom stats]], and presumably [[Derpy]]'s MOAR SKILLZ!!! perk.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 9, 2020|Added the event for the first anniversary, named Party Time! ([[SkyBlock Anniversary]]).|
|Jun 11, 2022|Extended the 3rd SkyBlock Anniversary from 2 days to 7 days.|

# Spooky Festival
Aliases: SF, Spooky Fishing, Spooky Sea Creatures, Spooky sea creatures
Categories: Events, Spooky Festival, Candy
[Spooky Festival]
Category: [[Events]]
Regular Festival Schedule: : Countdown:
Regular Spooky Fishing Schedule: : Countdown:
Foxy‘s Festival Schedule: : Countdown:
Foxy‘s Spooky Fishing Schedule: : Countdown:
The Spooky Festival is an [[Events|event]] that takes place from 29 of Autumn to 31 of Autumn. It lasts for 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]], during which monsters will have a chance to drop either [[Green Candy|Green]] or [[Purple Candy]].
## Mechanics
### Candy
All [[Mob|Mobs]] have a 10% chance to drop [[Green Candy]] when killed during a Spooky Festival. This chance can be increased with certain bonuses:
Increasing Green Candy Chance:
|Slot|Name|Extra Chance|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Helmet ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Chestplate ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Leggings ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Boots ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Helmet ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Chestplate ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Leggings ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Boots ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Candy Talisman]]|[[Candy Talisman]]|+5%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Ring]]|[[Candy Ring]]|+10%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Artifact]]|[[Candy Artifact]]|+15%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Relic]]|[[Candy Relic]]|+20%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Corn]]|Candied ([[Reforging]]) Reforge|+1%|Can be applied to all 4 [[Armor]] pieces to grant +4% total.|
|[[Bat Pet]]|[[Bat Pet]]|+0.1% → +20%|Chance increases with higher pet ([[Bat Pet]]) level.|
|Candy Scavenger I|Candy Scavenger ([[Doug]]) Perk|+2% → +10%|Chance increases with a higher perk level, up to level 5.|
||[[Foxy]] Sweet Benevolence perk|+30%||
With all of these upgrades, the base 10% candy chance increases to a 94% chance, or a 124% chance with [[Foxy]]'s perk. This chance is not increased by [[Magic Find]].
When [[Green Candy]] is obtained, there is a base 10% chance for it to be converted to [[Purple Candy]] instead. This conversion chance is increased with the following bonuses, which is also not affected by [[Magic Find]]:
Increasing Purple Candy Chance:
|Slot|Name|Extra Chance|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
||[[Mob]] Level|+0.2% per level|For example, a [Lv10] mob has a +2% conversion chance.|
|Purple Vogue I|Purple Vogue ([[Doug]]) Perk|+1% → +3%|Chance increases with a higher perk level, up to level 3.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|[[Spooky Armor]]|5%|From the Full Set Bonus|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|[[Bat Person Armor]]|5%|From the Full Set Bonus|
### Spooky Mobs
[[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]] spawn during the night whilst a Spooky Festival is taking place and drop [[Candy (disambiguation)|Candy]] when killed.
They spawn in the following [[Locations]]:
- [[Graveyard]]
- [[Wilderness]]
- [[Spider's Den]]
- [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]
### Trick or Treat Chests
When a [[Mob]] is killed during a Spooky Festival, there is a chance for a [[Trick or Treat Chest]] to spawn. The chance is 1% for normal Mobs and 3% for Spooky Mobs. The chance to drop Trick or Treat chests can be increased with the [[Lucky Hoof]] or [[Eternal Hoof]] accessories and with [[Doug]] [[Doug|Trick or Treat Enthusiast]] perk. Maximum chance to spawn is 4% and 6% for Spooky Mobs.
They can be opened to give the player either a TRICK or TREAT, the former spawning a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] and the latter granting the player 1-3 items.
#### Party Chests
When [[Foxy]]'s A Time for Giving perk is active, [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] spawn instead of Trick or Treat Chests. They cannot spawn [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], drop 2-4 items and the items drop in larger quantities as well as having some unique loot.
### Spooky Fishing
During Spooky Festivals, a new pool of [[Sea Creatures]] is added, which has a base 15% chance to override any normal [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] catch in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]]. The chance can also be increased by certain modifiers:
|Modifier|Chance (up to 75%)|
|-|-|
|Spooky Savant V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The [[Sea Creatures]] available from Spooky Festivals are listed below:
|Sea Creature|Mob Type|Fishing Skill|Base Weight|Base Chance|Categories|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jumpin' Jack]] (COMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]]|6|5000|50%|Liquid: Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) Island: Any Island Special: Spooky Festival|
|[[Scarecrow]] (UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]]|9|2875|28.75%|(same as above)|
|[[Nightmare]] (RARE ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]], Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|14|1500|15%|(same as above)|
|[[Werewolf]] (EPIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]], [[Animal]]|17|500|5%|(same as above)|
|[[Phantom Fisher]] (LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]], [[Elusive]]|21|100|1%|(same as above)|
|[[Grim Reaper]] (MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Spooky]], [[Elusive]]|26|25|0.25%|(same as above)|
### Rewards
The leaderboard is defined by the number of candy collected. It can be found next to the [[Fear Mongerer]].
#### Points
[[Green Candy]] grants 1 point, and [[Purple Candy]] grants 5 points towards the player's Candy Score. Candy obtained from [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]] does not count towards the leaderboard.
#### Rewards
Rewards will be given at the end of the Spooky Festival based on how many points the player obtained. Rewards can be claimed at the end of the event from the [[Fear Mongerer]] or from Event Rewards found in the [[Calendar]]. Unclaimed rewards are only claimable for 10 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Years]] from the year they are earned.
|Bracket Name|Bracket Points|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Top 50|Various|+ [[Golden Treat]]; +1,000,000 [[Coins]]; + [[Skeleton Horse Pet]]; + [[Witch Mask]]; + Enchanted Book (Vampirism VI) ([[Enchanted Book]]); + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 50 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Emerald|5000|+1,000,000 [[Coins]]; + [[Skeleton Horse Pet]]; + [[Witch Mask]]; + Enchanted Book (Vampirism VI) ([[Enchanted Book]]); + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 35 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Diamond|2000|+500,000 [[Coins]]; + [[Witch Mask]]; + Enchanted Book (Vampirism VI) ([[Enchanted Book]]); + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 25 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Gold|1750|+200,000 [[Coins]]; + Enchanted Book (Vampirism VI) ([[Enchanted Book]]); + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 20 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Iron|1500|+100,000 [[Coins]]; + Enchanted Book (Vampirism VI) ([[Enchanted Book]]); + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 15 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Stone|1000|+50,000 [[Coins]]; + [[Spooky Disc]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 10 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
|Wood|100|+25,000 [[Coins]]; + [[Spooky Pie]]; + 5 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event Starts|SPOOKY FESTIVAL has started! Mobs everywhere has a chance to drop Candy!|
|During Event|SPOOKY FESTIVAL The event is active! Kill mobs to collect Candy, which can be traded in for cool items in the Village!|
|[[Bat Piñata]] Spawned|SPOOKY FESTIVAL A Bat Piñata has appeared! It's dropping candies!|
|[[Bat Firework]] Launched|[player] launched a Bat Firework in the Village. It's dropping candies for everyone!|
|Event Ends|SPOOKY FESTIVAL The event has ended! The Fear Mongerer is selling cool items in the Hub for a limited time! Candy Contest results will arrive shortly.|
|Event Canceled|The Bonus Spooky Festival has been cancelled! Keep an eye on the Calendar to see the latest events and cancellations!|
## Trivia
- During Halloween in real life, additional Spooky Festivals are organized.
- During [[Dante]]'s term as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], the Spooky Festival, among other events, was canceled.
- Candy Score is capped at 5,000 on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profiles.
  - This cap was added after several instances of [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] players using tricks to obtain over 100,000 Candy Score, which is not feasible to the same extent on regular profiles.

# Starlyn Contest
Aliases: Agatha Contest, Agatha contest, Agatha's Contest, Miria's Contest, Starlyn, Starlyn Contests
Categories: Events, Foraging
[Starlyn Contest]
Starlyn Contests are an [[Events|event]] that take place every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]] and last for 20 real life minutes. During this event, players collect points by chopping trees, collecting island resources, and [[Hunting]] various creatures on the [[Moonglade Marsh]] and/or [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Moonglade Marsh Contests
Agatha's Contests take place on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], the first island of [[Galatea]]. They are hosted by [[Agatha]], who can be found in the center of the island. To speak to her, the player must have finished speaking to [[Swoop]] to learn about [[Sweep]] and [[Tree Gifts]], [[David Hunterborough]] to learn about [[Hunting]], and [[Kysha]] to learn about [[Attribute Fusion]].
### Participating
Points for a Agatha's Contest can be gained in various ways.
|Activity|Method|Points Given|
|-|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|Per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|10|
|Cutting Trees|Per 100 (1 stack plus 36) Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]])|3|
|Cutting Trees|Per 100 Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]])|6|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Verdant]]|3|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Azure]]|3|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Mossybit]]|6|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Cod]]|6|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Coralot]]|15|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Salmon]]|15|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Lumisquid]]|15|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Spike]]|15|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Shellwise]]|45|
|Hunting Creatures|[[Mudworm Shard]]|45|
|Hunting Creatures|Any other creature|30|
|Fishing|Per Sea Creature|10|
|Fishing|Per Treasure|25|
|Fishing|Per Catch|4|
|Combat|Per Creature Killed|8|
|Gathering Resources|Per [[Lushlilac]]|9|
|Gathering Resources|Per [[Sea Lumies]]|2|
### Rewards
There are 5 main reward brackets and 3 hidden ones, each corresponding to a rarity. Each bracket grants better rewards than the previous and depends on how many points were earned.
Additionally, the first 3 contests of each real-life day reward 1,200 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP. This is replaced by 1,200 [[Forest Whispers]] at HotF Level 8.
Reaching the [[Rarity|EPIC]] bracket grants a 10% chance to get a [[Agatha's Prize]], while reaching the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] bracket or above grants a 20% chance to get a [[Agatha's Prize]].
|Bracket|Points Required|Guaranteed Rewards|Additional Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|25|10 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 2,500 [[Forest Whispers]] 10 [[Forest Essence]] 10,000 [[Foraging]] XP 100 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 15 [[Bits]]||
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250|15 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 5,000 [[Forest Whispers]] 20 [[Forest Essence]] 20,000 [[Foraging]] XP 200 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 30 [[Bits]]||
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1,000|20 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 7,500 [[Forest Whispers]] 30 [[Forest Essence]] 30,000 [[Foraging]] XP 350 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 45 [[Bits]]||
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|2,000|25 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 10,500 [[Forest Whispers]] 40 [[Forest Essence]] 40,000 [[Foraging]] XP 500 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 60 [[Bits]]|1 [[Agatha's Prize]] (10%)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|3,000|30 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 12,500 [[Forest Whispers]] 50 [[Forest Essence]] 50,000 [[Foraging]] XP 700 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 75 [[Bits]]|1 [[Agatha's Prize]] (20%)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|5,000|35 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 15,000 [[Forest Whispers]] 60 [[Forest Essence]] 50,000 [[Foraging]] XP 700 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 90 [[Bits]]|1 [[Agatha's Prize]] (20%)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|8,000|40 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 17,500 [[Forest Whispers]] 70 (1 stack plus 6) [[Forest Essence]] 50,000 [[Foraging]] XP 750 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 105 [[Bits]]|1 [[Agatha's Prize]] (20%)|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|12,000|45 [[Agatha's Coupon]] 20,000 [[Forest Whispers]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Forest Essence]] 50,000 [[Foraging]] XP 750 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 120 [[Bits]]|1 [[Agatha's Prize]] (20%)|
## Torrhus Canyon Contests
Miria's Contests take place on the [[Torrhus Canyon]], the second island of [[Galatea]]. They are hosted by [[Miria]], who can be found in the center of the island. Like on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], these contests also last for 20 minutes.
### Participating
Points for a Miria's Contest can be gained in various ways.
|Activity|Method|Points Given|
|-|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|Per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|10|
|Cutting Trees|Per 100 Helix Logs ([[Helix Log]])|9|
|Hunting Creatures|Per COMMON ([[Rarity]]) Shard ([[Attributes]])|9|
|Hunting Creatures|Per UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Shard ([[Attributes]])|25|
|Hunting Creatures|Per RARE ([[Rarity]]) Shard ([[Attributes]])|50|
|Hunting Creatures|Per EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Shard ([[Attributes]])|100|
|Hunting Creatures|Per LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Shard ([[Attributes]])|200|
|Fishing|Per Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|10|
|Fishing|Per Catch|10|
|Fishing|Per GOOD [[Treasure]] Catch|50|
|Fishing|Per GREAT [[Treasure]] Catch|250|
|Fishing|Per OUTSTANDING [[Treasure]] Catch|750|
|Combat|Per Creature Killed|4|
|Gathering Resources|Per [[Honeycomb]]|1|
|Gathering Resources|Per [[Ruby Veilshroom]]|8|
### Rewards
There are 5 main reward brackets and 3 hidden ones, each corresponding to a rarity. Each bracket grants better rewards than the previous and depends on how many points were earned.
Additionally, the first 3 contests of each real-life day reward 1,200 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP. This is replaced by 1,200 [[Desert Whispers]] at HotF Level 8.
Reaching the [[Rarity|EPIC]] bracket grants a 10% chance to get a [[Miria's Prize]], while reaching the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] bracket or above grants a 20% chance to get a [[Miria's Prize]].
|Bracket|Points Required|Guaranteed Rewards|Additional Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|50|10 [[Miria's Coupon]] 20 [[Forest Essence]] +15,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 150 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP 3,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 15 [[Bits]]||
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250|15 [[Miria's Coupon]] 30 [[Forest Essence]] +30,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 250 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP Basic Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 5,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 30 [[Bits]]||
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1,000|20 [[Miria's Coupon]] 40 [[Forest Essence]] +45,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 400 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP Economy Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 8,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 45 [[Bits]]||
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|2,500|25 [[Miria's Coupon]] 50 [[Forest Essence]] +60,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 550 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP Premium Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 12,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 60 [[Bits]]|1 [[Miria's Prize]] (10%)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5,000|30 [[Miria's Coupon]] 50 [[Forest Essence]] +75,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 900 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP First-Class Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 18,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 75 [[Bits]]|1 [[Miria's Prize]] (20%)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|10,000|35 [[Miria's Coupon]] 70 [[Forest Essence]] +75,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 900 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP First-Class Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 24,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 90 [[Bits]]|1 [[Miria's Prize]] (20%)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|15,000|40 [[Miria's Coupon]] 80 [[Forest Essence]] +75,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 900 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP First-Class Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 30,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 105 [[Bits]]|1 [[Miria's Prize]] (20%)|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]); (hidden)|20,000|45 [[Miria's Coupon]] 90 (1 stack plus 26) [[Forest Essence]] +75,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]) 900 [[Heart of the Forest]] XP First-Class Safari Ticket ([[Safari Tickets]]) 36,000 [[Desert Whispers]] 120 [[Bits]]|1 [[Miria's Prize]] (20%)|
## Notes
- The [[Foraging]] XP gained is not affected by [[Foraging Wisdom]]. It is also not affected by XP boosts caused by the [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] or [[Raffle of the Century|Century Celebration]].
## Tips
- For Moonglade Marsh Contest, with the [[Attributes|Berry Enjoyer X]] attribute, obtained from [[Bullfrog Shard|Bullfrog Shards]], and harvesting [[Lushlilac]] bushes and catching any [[Birries]] that spawn, players can easily get high brackets from the event, without needing expensive setups or dedicating too much time to the event.
  - Note that additional [[Lushlilac]] and [[Sea Lumies]] from the [[Attributes|Yummy]] attribute will not count for point gains.
- For Torrhus Canyon Contest, diving into the [[Torrhus Springs]] to hunt [[Solar]], [[Ember]] and harvest [[Ruby Veilshroom]] in the air pockets to the side, players can easily get high brackets.
  - A decent amount of [[Pressure Resistance]] is required to be able to reach the third [[Ruby Veilshroom]] cave and to more reliably hunt the [[Ember]].
- Additional logs from [[Foraging Fortune]] will count toward the 100 logs requirement to gain points for logs.
## Trivia
- Points from Hunting Creatures are gained per mob instead of per shard, meaning [[Hunting Fortune]] does not affect point gain.
- Unlike [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Contests]], servers can restart during Starlyn Contests.

# Fishing Hotspots
Aliases: Fishing Hotspot
Categories: Fishing
[Fishing Hotspots]
Fishing Hotspots, or Hotspots, are a [[Fishing]] related mechanic that appear on certain [[Locations|Public Islands]]. Hotspots appear as rings in various locations and grant perks to players who fish in them.
## Mechanics
### Unlocking
Hotspots begin spawning on certain [[Locations|Public Islands]] after speaking to [[Hattie]] in the [[Backwater Bayou]].
### Locations
Certain Public Islands contain a set number of Hotspots. Each Hotspot lasts for about 4 minutes before moving. Each island has a certain number of Hotspots at once. Hotspots appear in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on most islands and in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
|Island|Number of Hotspots|
|-|-|
|[[Spider's Den]]|1|
|[[The Park]]|1|
|[[Backwater Bayou]]|2|
|[[Jerry's Workshop]]|2|
|[[Lotus Atoll]]|2|
|[[Torrhus Canyon]]|2|
|[[Hub Island]]|3|
|[[Crimson Isle]]|3|
## Usage
### Stats
Fishing in a Hotspot grants the player one of the following stats:
- +15 [[Fishing Speed]]
- +5 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- +2 [[Double Hook Chance]]
- +5 [[Trophy Chance]]
- +1 [[Treasure Chance]]
Stats granted from Fishing Hotspots are doubled if a [[Hotspot Sinker]] is applied to the player's [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
Additionally, the Hotspot in the [[Hotspot Haven]] grants the Chance to catch Giant Water Bug Shard effect, replacing all [[Treasure|GREAT CATCHES]] and [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCHES]] with varying quantities of [[Giant Water Bug Shard|Giant Water Bug Shards]]: this is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Sea Creatures
Additionally, Hotspots allow certain [[Sea Creatures]] to be caught:
|Sea Creature|Mob Type|Fishing Skill|Base Weight|Base Chance|Categories|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Frog Man]] (COMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]], [[Humanoid]]|5|5000|50%|Liquid: Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) Island: Any Island Special: Hotspot|
|[[Inkling]] (UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]]|7|2875|28.75%|(same as above)|
|[[Snapping Turtle]] (RARE ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]]|10|1500|15%|(same as above)|
|[[Manta Ray]] (EPIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]]|19|500|5%|(same as above)|
|[[Blue Ringed Octopus]] (LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]], [[Elusive]]|35|100|1%|(same as above)|
|[[Wiki Tiki]] (MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Construct]], [[Elusive]]|40|25|0.25%|(same as above)|
|[[Fried Chicken]] (COMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], [[Animal]]|14|5000|50%|Liquid: Lava ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) Island: [[Crimson Isle]] Special: Hotspot|
|[[Volcanic Snail]] (UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], [[Animal]]|17|2875|28.75%|(same as above)|
|[[Fireproof Witch]] (RARE ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], [[Humanoid]], [[Arcane]]|22|1500|15%|(same as above)|
|[[Magma Pillar]] (EPIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]])|31|500|5%|(same as above)|
|[[Fiery Scuttler]] (LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], [[Animal]], [[Elusive]]|37|100|1%|(same as above)|
|[[Ragnarok]] (MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Magmatic]], [[Skeletal]], [[Elusive]]|47|25|0.25%|(same as above)|
Hotspot Sea Creatures have a base chance of 15% (1 in 6.67) to replace a regular Sea Creature catch. The chances can be increased through certain sources:
|Source|Increase|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
Combined, these sources can increase the chance to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures to 85%.
## Gallery

- A Hotspot in lava
- A Hotspot in the Hub's Fishing Outpost A Hotspot in the Hub's [[Fishing Outpost]]
- A Hotspot in the Hub's Fairy Pond A Hotspot in the Hub's [[Wilderness|Fairy Pond]]
- A Hotspot in the Birch Park A Hotspot in the [[Birch Park]]
- A Hotspot in the Spider's Den A Hotspot in the [[Spider's Den]]
- A Hotspot in the Backwater Bayou A Hotspot in the [[Backwater Bayou]]
- A Hotspot in the Lotus Atoll A Hotspot in the [[Lotus Atoll]]
- A Hotspot in the Crimson Isle A Hotspot in the [[Crimson Isle]]

# Year of the Seal
Aliases: Seal, Seal Year, Seal year, YotS
Categories: Fishing, Year of the Seal
Year of the Seal
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Year of the Seal is an [[Events|Event]] that happens every 12 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Years]].
## Mechanics
During the Year of the Seal, [[Lukas the Aquarist]] appears in the [[Village]] and players can fish up [[Bouncy Beach Ball|Bouncy Beach Balls]] and [[Giant Bouncy Beach Ball|Giant Bouncy Beach Balls]].
### Beach Ball Minigame
Player can play the Beach Ball Minigame to obtain [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].
### Shop
[[Lukas the Aquarist]] sells fishing-related items in exchange for [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].
## Tips
- Since [[Bouncy Beach Ball|Bouncy Beach Balls]] and [[Giant Bouncy Beach Ball|Giant Bouncy Beach Balls]] replace Treasure from fishing, it is recommended to fish in the [[Backwater Bayou]], as there are sizeable sources of [[Treasure Chance]] along with a low [[Fishing Speed]] cap.

You spot a fin as blue as the water it came from, it's a Blue Shark.
Blue Shark is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]].
## Spawning
Blue Shark can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Shark Sonar V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Shark Bait]]|+20%|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Megalodon Pet]]|+20%|
|[[Shark Scale Armor]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Blue Shark is 1,500, resulting in a catch chance of 30.15% (1 in 3.32 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You spot a fin as blue as the water it came from, it's a Blue Shark.|
## Behavior
They chase after their target like a normal [[Zombie]] but are a bit faster.

# Great White Shark

[Great White Shark]

Mob Level: 160
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]] [[Elusive]]

[Fishing Info]
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 100
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XXIV (24) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Shark Bait]] [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
Event Required: [[Fishing Festival]]
[Drops]
Drops: 16x [[Shark Fin]] 0–1x LEGENDARY Megalodon Pet ([[Megalodon Pet]]) 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]] 0–1x [[Great White Shark Tooth]] 0–1x [[Carnival Ticket]]
XP: +3,200 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 401
[Metadata]
ID: GREAT_WHITE_SHARK_180
Hide no longer, a Great White Shark has tracked your scent and thirsts for your blood!
Great White Shark is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]].
## Spawning
Great White Shark can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXIV (24)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Shark Sonar V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Shark Bait]]|+20%|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Megalodon Pet]]|+20%|
|[[Shark Scale Armor]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Great White Shark is 100, resulting in a catch chance of 2.01% (1 in 49.75 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Hide no longer, a Great White Shark has tracked your scent and thirsts for your blood!|
## Behavior
The Great White Shark chases after their target like a normal [[Zombie]], but are a bit faster. They also shoot teeth at their target.

The Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It increases the player's [[Strength]] by 2 and grants a +5% chance to get Shark Teeth during [[Fishing Festival|Fishing Festivals.]]
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] 8x [[Shark Fin]]|Recipe: [[Shark Fin]] ×8 + [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] → Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dull Shark Tooth Necklace]]|None|[[Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace]] 1x Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace 4x [[Nurse Shark Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] ×4 + Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace → [[Dull Shark Tooth Necklace]]|
## Tips
- It is recommended to get this talisman for beginners because of its cheap price.
## Trivia
- The Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace references Terraria's Shark Tooth Necklace.

Shark Fin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item which is obtained by killing [[Sharks (disambiguation)|Sharks]] during the [[Fishing Festival]]. It is used to craft [[Shark Bait]] and the [[Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace]].
Its enchanted form is used to craft [[Shark Scale Armor]] and the [[Rod of the Sea]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv160]|[[Great White Shark]]|16|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv99]|[[Tiger Shark]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv52]|[[Blue Shark]]|4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv22]|[[Nurse Shark]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
Sharks have up to a 100% chance to drop an extra Shark Fin by unlocking the Elasmobranchii Affinity perk at [[Doug]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Shark Fin]]|None|[[Shark Fin]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Shark Fin|Recipe: Shark Fin x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Shark Fin]]|
|[[Shark Bait]]|None|[[Shark Fin]] 2x Shark Fin|Recipe: Shark Fin ×2 → [[Shark Bait]] x16|
|[[Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace]]|None|1x [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] [[Shark Fin]] 8x Shark Fin|Recipe: Shark Fin ×8 + [[Nurse Shark Tooth]] → [[Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace]]|

## Trivia
- It grants the fourth highest amount of base [[Sea Creature Chance]] in the game, at 2.5% per piece and 10% total. It also grants the highest [[Sea Creature Chance]] out of fishing sets that can be obtained from water fishing, with the higher tiers of armor, [[Lava Sea Creature Armor]], [[Thunder Armor]] and [[Magma Lord Armor]] only being obtained through lava fishing.
- Wearing the full set will unlock the [[Achievements|"Absorb it all!" Achievement]] which normally unlocks by wearing full set of [[Sponge Armor]].
- There used to be a bug where if a player wears [[Sponge Armor]] or Shark Scale Armor in water, then switches to another armor set while still in the water, they can gain the 2x [[Defense]] buff even if they are not wearing Sponge Armor or Shark Scale Armor.
- If the player wears this set and has a [[Fishing]] level of less than 24, the Full Set Bonus Absorb still doubles the player's [[Defense]].

# Tiger Shark
Categories: Fishing Festival, Sea Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Fishing, Mobs
Fishing Festival Exclusive Tiger Shark is only obtainable during [[Fishing Festival]].
[Tiger Shark]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 99
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 250,000; [[Damage]]: 300
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 500
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XVIII (18) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Shark Bait]] [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
Event Required: [[Fishing Festival]]
[Drops]
Drops: 8x [[Shark Fin]] 0–1x [[Tiger Shark Tooth]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]] 0–1x EPIC Megalodon Pet ([[Megalodon Pet]]) 0–1x [[Carnival Ticket]]
XP: +1,485 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 149
[Metadata]
ID: TIGER_SHARK_50
A striped beast bounds from the depths, the wild Tiger Shark!
Tiger Shark is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]].
## Spawning
Tiger Shark can be fished up from water during the [[Fishing Festival]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XVIII (18)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Shark Sonar V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Shark Bait]]|+20%|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Megalodon Pet]]|+20%|
|[[Shark Scale Armor]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Tiger Shark is 500, resulting in a catch chance of 10.05% (1 in 9.95 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Fishing Festival Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A striped beast bounds from the depths, the wild Tiger Shark!|
## Behavior
Tiger Sharks chase after their target like a normal [[Zombie]], but are a bit faster. They can also shoot teeth at the player.

|[[Aquamarine Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/2.5M|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POWER_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Gemstones:; [] [] [] [] [] [] []; Allows you to harness the power of 7 Gemstones, although only half of the normal power can be harnessed!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Artifact of Power is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Ring of Power]] and upgrades into the [[Relic of Power]], and is unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone X (10)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gemstone X (10) ([[Gemstone]])|1x [[Ring of Power]] 32x [[Gemstone Mixture]]|Recipe: [[Gemstone Mixture]] x4 ×8 + [[Ring of Power]] → Artifact of Power|
## Usage
The Artifact of Power can have 7 [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to it in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]]. Applied Gemstones provide half of the stats they would usually give.
The following Gemstones can be placed in the Artifact of Power:
- [[Gemstone|Jade]]
- [[Gemstone|Amber]]
- [[Gemstone|Topaz]]
- [[Gemstone|Sapphire]]
- [[Gemstone|Amethyst]]
- [[Gemstone|Jasper]]
- [[Gemstone|Ruby]]
When all 7 Perfect Gemstones are applied to an Artifact of Power, its rarity is upgraded from [[Rarity|RARE]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]].

Item ID: CACTUS_KNIFE_3
Tooltip: Farming Tool; Slices through cactus instantly!; Ability: Swift Slicer Increases your speed cap by 100; while in The Garden.; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 7.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
The Cactus Knife is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Cactus]]. It can break [[Cactus]] instantly, and increases the player's [[Speed]] Cap by 100 while held on [[The Garden]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
A Cactus Knife Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Cactus Knife Mk. II ([[Cactus Knife]])|None|1x Cactus Knife Mk. I ([[Cactus Knife]]) 32x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] x8 ×4 + [[Cactus Knife]] → [[Cactus Knife]]|
|Cactus Knife Mk. III ([[Cactus Knife]])|None|1x Cactus Knife Mk. II ([[Cactus Knife]]) 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Cactus]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] x32 ×4 + [[Cactus Knife]] → [[Cactus Knife]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Cactus Knife Mk. I ([[Cactus Knife]])
[Effects]
[Slices through Cactus Cactus instantly! Ability: Swift Slicer; Increases your speed cap by 100 while in The Garden.]
Farming Tool:
Cactus Knife Mk. II ([[Cactus Knife]])
[Effects]
[Slices through Cactus Cactus instantly! Ability: Swift Slicer; Increases your speed cap by 100 while in The Garden.]
Farming Tool:
Cactus Knife Mk. III ([[Cactus Knife]])
[Effects]
[Slices through Cactus Cactus instantly! Ability: Swift Slicer; Increases your speed cap by 100 while in The Garden.]
The Cactus Knife allows the player to instantly break [[Cactus]], it also increases the player's [[Speed]] cap by 100, which allows a particular farm design.
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Cactus Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

## Trivia
- Since vanilla Minecraft does not provide a way to insta-break cactus in survival mode, the Cactus Knife's ability is implemented server-side, meaning this means there is a one tick delay before the cactus breaks. Therefore, you cannot break multiple layers of cactus at once without losing efficiency.
  - This means that maximum efficiency when farming cactus requires 464 [[Speed]].

## Trivia
- The Cocoa Chopper was originally a wooden axe which only allowed hoe enchants at release, making it impossible to enchant with efficiency, and so was unable to instabreak [[Cocoa Beans]]. A patch allowed the Cocoa Chopper to be enchanted with efficiency, but, as it was still a wood axe, it was still unable to instabreak cocoa beans without haste. A third patch changed the Cocoa Chopper to a gold axe, allowing it to break cocoa instantly. A fourth patch removed the ability to auto-replenish fully-grown cocoa beans, and added a new ability.
- The auto-replanting ability was removed on December 3rd, 2020. It was replaced with a 20% boost to cocoa bean output, making it more viable than a [[Treecapitator]] for farming [[Cocoa Beans]].

Eclipse Sickle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]] and [[Moonflower|Moonflowers]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
The Eclipse Sickle Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Eclipse Sickle Mk. II ([[Eclipse Sickle]])|None|1x Eclipse Sickle Mk. I ([[Eclipse Sickle]]) 20x [[Compacted Moonflower]] 20x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x10 ×2 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x10 ×2 + [[Eclipse Sickle]] → [[Eclipse Sickle]]|
|Eclipse Sickle Mk. III ([[Eclipse Sickle]])|None|1x Eclipse Sickle Mk. II ([[Eclipse Sickle]]) 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Compacted Sunflower]] 80x [[Compacted Moonflower]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x40 ×2 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x40 ×2 + [[Eclipse Sickle]] → [[Eclipse Sickle]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Eclipse Sickle Mk. I ([[Eclipse Sickle]])
Farming Tool:
Eclipse Sickle Mk. II ([[Eclipse Sickle]])
Farming Tool:
Eclipse Sickle Mk. III ([[Eclipse Sickle]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Sunflower Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Moonflower Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

## Trivia
- Euclid's Wheat Sickle is a reference to Greek mathematician Euclid.

Tooltip: Farming Tool; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 9.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
The Fungi Cutter Mk. I is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Mushroom|Red Mushrooms]] and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
A Fungi Cutter Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Fungi Cutter Mk. II ([[Fungi Cutter]])|None|1x Fungi Cutter Mk. I ([[Fungi Cutter]]) 10x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 10x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x5 ×2 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x5 ×2 + [[Fungi Cutter]] → [[Fungi Cutter]]|
|Fungi Cutter Mk. III ([[Fungi Cutter]])|None|1x Fungi Cutter Mk. II ([[Fungi Cutter]]) 40x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] 40x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x20 ×2 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] x20 ×2 + [[Fungi Cutter]] → [[Fungi Cutter]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Fungi Cutter Mk. I ([[Fungi Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Fungi Cutter Mk. II ([[Fungi Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Fungi Cutter Mk. III ([[Fungi Cutter]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Mushroom Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

## Trivia
- The Fungi Cutter's ability does not include mushroom blocks.
- Despite being classed as a fungus, [[Mycelium]] and [[Nether Wart]] are not included in the item's ability.

## Trivia
- Gauss Carrot Shovel is a reference to German mathematician and physician Carl Friedrich Gauss.

Tooltip: Farming Tool; Melon Slice Fortune: +4; Farming Wisdom: +1; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 6.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
The Melon Dicer is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] specialized for farming [[Melon|Melons]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
### SkyMart
A Melon Dicer Mk. I can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Melon Dicer Mk. II ([[Melon Dicer]])|None|1x Melon Dicer Mk. I ([[Melon Dicer]]) 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]] 64x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x16 ×4 + [[Melon Dicer]] → [[Melon Dicer]]|
|Melon Dicer Mk. III ([[Melon Dicer]])|None|1x Melon Dicer Mk. II ([[Melon Dicer]]) 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]] 256x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x64 ×4 + [[Melon Dicer]] → [[Melon Dicer]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Melon Dicer Mk. I ([[Melon Dicer]])
Farming Tool:
Melon Dicer Mk. II ([[Melon Dicer]])
Farming Tool:
Melon Dicer Mk. III ([[Melon Dicer]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Melon Slice Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

Newton Nether Wart Cutter is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Nether Wart|Nether Warts]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
The Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. II ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]])|None|1x Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. I ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]) 60x [[Mutant Nether Wart]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Mutant Nether Wart]] x15 ×4 + [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]] → [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]|
|Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. III ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]])|None|1x Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. II ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]) 240x (3 stacks plus 48 (240 in total)) [[Mutant Nether Wart]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Mutant Nether Wart]] x60 ×4 + [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]] → [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. I ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. II ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Newton Nether Wart Cutter Mk. III ([[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Nether Wart Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

## Trivia
- Newton Nether Wart Cutter is a reference to English mathematician, physicist, astronomer and alchemist Isaac Newton, best known for his 'discovery' of gravity as well as the 3 laws of motion.

Perfect Armor is a tiered [[Rarity|RARE]]-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is unlocked at [[Diamond|Diamond IX (9)]] ( 50,000 (781 stacks plus 16) [[Diamond|Diamonds]]).
## Obtaining
### Crafting
#### Tier I
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Diamond IX (9) ([[Diamond]])|5x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×5 → Perfect Helmet - Tier I|
|Diamond IX (9) ([[Diamond]])|8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 → Perfect Chestplate - Tier I|
|Diamond IX (9) ([[Diamond]])|7x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×7 → Perfect Leggings - Tier I|
|Diamond IX (9) ([[Diamond]])|4x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×4 → Perfect Boots - Tier I|
### Upgrading
Upgrading each piece of Perfect Armor by one tier requires 4 [[Enchanted Diamond Block|Enchanted Diamond Blocks]] (16 per set).
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Diamond IX (9) ([[Diamond]])|4 Enchanted Diamond Blocks ([[Enchanted Diamond Block]]) 1x piece of [[Perfect Armor]] Perfect Armor (any tier)|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×4 + Perfect Helmet - Tier I/Perfect Chestplate - Tier I/Perfect Leggings - Tier I/Perfect Boots - Tier I → Perfect Helmet - Tier II/Perfect Chestplate - Tier II/Perfect Leggings - Tier II/Perfect Boots - Tier II|
1 [[Perfectly Cut Diamond]] can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with any Perfect Armor Tier XII armor piece to upgrade it to Tier XIII.

## Tips
- A high-tier Perfect Chestplate or Perfect Boots can be used by [[Mage|Mages]] in [[Dungeons]] for higher [[Effective Health]].
- Tier XIII Perfect Boots typically grant similar or higher [[Effective Health]] than [[Goldor's Armor|Goldor's Boots]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- When paired with a [[Turtle Pet]], the player can gain massive [[Defense]].
- While it is generally better to go for [[Armor]] that increases [[Damage]], using Perfect Boots may be viable to reach 1,000 [[Defense]] to gain the full bonus of an [[Ankylosaurus Pet]].
## Trivia
- When reforged with a [[Diamond Atom]], the armor prefix becomes Absolutely Perfect instead of Perfect.
- Perfect Armor emits more sparkling particles as its tier increases.

Tooltip: Farming Tool; Pumpkin Fortune: +4; Farming Wisdom: +1; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 4.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
The Pumpkin Dicer is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
A Pumpkin Dicer Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Pumpkin Dicer Mk. II ([[Pumpkin Dicer]])|None|1x Pumpkin Dicer Mk. I ([[Pumpkin Dicer]]) 20x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x5 ×4 + [[Pumpkin Dicer]] → [[Pumpkin Dicer]]|
|Pumpkin Dicer Mk. III ([[Pumpkin Dicer]])|None|1x Pumpkin Dicer Mk. II ([[Pumpkin Dicer]]) 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Polished Pumpkin]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x20 ×4 + [[Pumpkin Dicer]] → [[Pumpkin Dicer]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Pumpkin Dicer Mk. I ([[Pumpkin Dicer]])
Farming Tool:
Pumpkin Dicer Mk. II ([[Pumpkin Dicer]])
Farming Tool:
Pumpkin Dicer Mk. III ([[Pumpkin Dicer]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Pumpkin Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Gemstones:; [] [] [] [] []; [] [] [] [] []; [] []; Allows you to harness the power of 12 Gemstones, although only half of the normal power can be harnessed!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 10.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Relic of Power is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Artifact of Power]], is the final form of the [[Talisman of Power]], and is unlocked at [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM X (10)]].
## Obtaining
A Relic of Power can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It takes 8 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Relic of Power]] 1 Relic of Power
  - 1 [[Artifact of Power]]
  - 1 [[Perfect Plate]] 1 [[Umber Plate]] 4 [[Refined Umber]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Umber]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Umber]] 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 256 (4 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 40,960 (640 stacks) [[Glacite]] 1 [[Tungsten Plate]] 4 [[Refined Tungsten]] 640 [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 102,400 [[Tungsten]] 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 256 [[Enchanted Glacite]] 40,960 [[Glacite]] 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 [[Iron Ingot]]
  - 32 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 128 (2 stacks) [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 10,240 (160 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 819,200 (12,800 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 128 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 10,240 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 819,200 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 128 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 10,240 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 819,200 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 128 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 10,240 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 819,200 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 8,192 (128 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 1,310,720 (20,480 stacks) [[Glacite]]
## Usage
The Relic of Power can have 12 [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to it in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]]. Applied Gemstones provide half of the stats they would usually give.
When all 12 Perfect Gemstones are applied to a Relic of Power, its rarity is upgraded from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Trivia
- Upgrading the Relic of Power to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] costs .
  - Upgrading from a fully maxed Artifact of Power costs .
- This is the only accessory of its upgrade line to have a requirement.

A Tormentor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]] that can be obtained from paid reward chests after defeating [[Kuudra]].
## Obtaining
A Tormentor has a 0.1% (1 in 972) chance to appear in the [[Kuudra|Infernal Kuudra]] paid chest.

Tooltip: Farming Tool; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
Turing Sugar Cane Cutter is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Sugar Cane]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
The Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. II ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]])|None|1x Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. I ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]) 40x [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] x10 ×4 + [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]] → [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]|
|Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. III ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]])|None|1x Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. II ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]) 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] x40 ×4 + [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]] → [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. I ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. II ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Turing Sugar Cane Cutter Mk. III ([[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Sugar Cane Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.

## Trivia
- Turing Sugar Cane Cutter is a reference to English mathematician and computer scientist Alan Turing, known for breaking the Enigma code during World War II, which allowed encoded German messages to be easily deciphered.

Tooltip: Farming Tool; Right-click to view recipes!; Level 1 -&gt; 2 (0\ 1k); 0%; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 10.; ❣ Requires Garden Level 12.; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
Wild Rose Hoe is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]–[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]], specialised for farming [[Wild Rose|Wild Roses]]. Its rarity can be upgraded by crafting higher tiers.
## Obtaining
The Wild Rose Cutter Mk. I can be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] on [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Wild Rose Cutter Mk. II ([[Wild Rose Cutter]])|None|1x Wild Rose Cutter Mk. I ([[Wild Rose Cutter]]) 40x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x16 ×4 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x10 ×4 + [[Wild Rose Cutter]] → [[Wild Rose Cutter]]|
|Wild Rose Cutter Mk. III ([[Wild Rose Cutter]])|None|1x Wild Rose Cutter Mk. II ([[Wild Rose Cutter]]) 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 256x [[Jacob's Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x40 ×4 + [[Wild Rose Cutter]] → [[Wild Rose Cutter]]|
## Usage
Farming Tool:
Wild Rose Cutter Mk. I ([[Wild Rose Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Wild Rose Cutter Mk. II ([[Wild Rose Cutter]])
Farming Tool:
Wild Rose Cutter Mk. III ([[Wild Rose Cutter]])
The Farming Tool level can be increased by gaining farming experience with it. Each level increases the amount of experience needed to obtain the proceeding level. Levels can increase the amount of [[Farming Fortune]], [[Crop Fortune|Wild Rose Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], [[Gemstone]] slots, and rarity.
After the tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max hoe level of 50.
After a tool reaches level 50, every 200,000 experience will drop a [[Tool Exp Capsule]], with no effect on the Farming Tool itself. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experience.
