# Blacklisted Items
Aliases: Blacklisted Item
Categories: Glitches, Blacklisted Items
Blacklisted items are generally items that were never meant to exist in the game, but unlike [[Glitched Items]], can impact the experience in one way or another. These items get removed from the player's inventory as soon as the server runs the next inventory check. This will remove it from your [[Ender Chest]], your [[Backpack|Backpacks]], containers on your island (including furniture), item frames, your [[Personal Vault]], and your inventory. This rule also applies to items with the same UUID to prevent duplication exploits.
When blacklisted items are removed from your inventory, the following message will appear in chat:
You had # blacklisted items in your <location>, they have been deleted! Sorry!
## Blacklisted items
Blacklisted items include the following:
|Item|Rarity|Image|
|-|-|-|
|[[Barrier]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|0th Year [[New Year Cake]]|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])||
|Written Book|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|[[Dragon Egg]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||
|Armor Stand ([[Vanilla Objects]])|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|[[Banner]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|Command Block|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|Overstacked items|Differs|[[Vanilla Objects/Music Discs]] x2 (example)|
|Items with the same UUID|Differs|[[Overflux Power Orb]] x2 (example)|
|Two or more SkyBlock Menus ([[SkyBlock Menu]]) or Quiver Arrows in invalid location.|None||
|[[Bingo Pet]] outside a bingo profile.|Differs||
|Book and Quill|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||
## History

|Oct 13, 2021|Added new container checks to remove blacklisted items.|

# Dragon Egg
Aliases: Dragon Egg (Item)
Categories: Glitches, Vanilla objects, Legendary items, Items
This item or page is a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]] or is referencing a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]]. More info may be found at the Glitched SkyBlock Items Wiki.
Dragon Egg is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Dragon Egg.
[Dragon Egg]
Type: Glitched Item ([[Glitched Items]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DRAGON_EGG
Tooltip: * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY
The Dragon Egg is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Glitched Items|glitched]] and [[Blacklisted Items|blacklisted]] item.
## Obtaining
A Dragon Egg was obtainable for a short period of time through setting an [[Ender Chest]] page to use a Dragon Egg as its display item, and then using chat macros to send both /enderchest and /ec at the same time, which would allow for the removal of the items within the [[Ender Chest]] display slots. Once obtained, it was as simple as double clicking on the obtained Dragon Egg to create many more of these items.
If stored within certain storage containers, such as [[Vanilla Objects|Item Frames]], it could be kept in item form without being removed.
## Trivia
- As it was once sold for 10,000 [[Coins]], people were able to make many of these eggs and gain large amounts of coins from selling them to [[Shops]]. However, many players who abused this were banned.
- It was first obtained after the admin Minikloon placed one down in a hub as a joke. It was able to be interacted with, teleporting around the hub, until it landed on a torch or similar transparent block, where it broke and was able to be obtained.
- Although the Dragon Egg is blacklisted, players may still place it on their islands.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|A player was able to break a Dragon Egg, placed by former admin 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) Minikloon in a hub, creating the first of its kind.|
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Dragon Eggs can be obtained through a bug in the [[Ender Chest]].|
|Unknown Date|Dragon Eggs are no longer obtainable and are blacklisted .|
|Jun 9, 2026|Made Dragon Egg Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jun 9, 2026|The base item of Dragon Egg is now [[Paper]], making it no longer placeable. However, the item model remains [[Dragon Egg]] Dragon Egg.|

# Glitched Items
Aliases: Book and Quill, Empty Rune, Green Lucky Block, Lucky Block, Null Head, Null Heads, Null Skull, Null Skulls, Red Lucky Block, Skeleton Skull, Tagless Items, Unobtainable Items, Yellow Lucky Block
Categories: Glitches, More info needed, Pages containing missing tooltips, Items
This item or page is a Glitched Item or is referencing a Glitched Item. More info may be found at the Glitched SkyBlock Items Wiki.
Although this page has been developed by many people, the information may be outdated or incorrect, and due to the very nature of this topic, misinformation is common. Please take care that all information is confirmed and up to date.
Unobtainable and Glitched Items can be created through glitches, and some can sell for high prices on the [[Auction House]]. Some items, such as Nether Stars and items without [[Rarity|Rarities]] cannot be put on the [[Auction House]]. The items serve no use other than trophy items, though some can have a larger effect, such as crashing player's games.
The terms "null" and "Missing Texture" are very different. "null" is the name for items with glitched IDs, or items that shouldn't exist within skyblock. "Missing Texture" is for items that are missing a texture, commonly shown as a purple and black item ( ).
## List of Glitched Items
### Notable Null Items
For more information, visit [[Null]].

## List of Blacklisted Items
Blacklisted Items are items that were not intended to exist and are periodically removed from all player inventories. These items are blacklisted by Hypixel and cannot be obtained by normal means. Possessing or creating blacklisted items is not punishable unless used maliciously or to gain an unfair advantage.

Iron Horse Armor, Gold Horse Armor, Diamond Horse Armor are discontinued, unobtainable items. They were previously obtained by [[Fishing]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
Horse Armor was obtainable through the means of fishing when [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]] was first released. This was patched after the first fishing update which removed vanilla fishing. The only way to acquire Horse Armor is through the [[Auction House]].
## Usage
Horse Armor has no use.
## Trivia
- Horse Armor cannot be equipped on a [[Horse Pet]].
- Gold Horse Armor and Diamond Horse Armor have the [[Rarity|EPIC]] rarity, while the Iron Horse Armor has [[Rarity|RARE]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 13, 2019|0.2|Horse Armor is obtainable through fishing.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Removed Horse Armors from fishing loot, making them no longer obtainable.|
|Jun 19, 2026|Horse Armors are now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|

# Spawn Egg
Aliases: Spawn Villager
Categories: Glitches, Common items, Items
This item or page is a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]] or is referencing a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]]. More info may be found at the Glitched SkyBlock Items Wiki.
[Spawn Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: Glitches
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MONSTER_EGG
Tooltip: COMMON
Spawn Egg or Spawn <Mob name> is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] glitched item.
## Obtaining
As of now, there are two types of Spawn Eggs in SkyBlock:
- Spawn Egg, an empty variant with white texture
- Spawn Villager, a villager spawn egg, not to be confused with [[Move Jerry]]
Spawn Eggs were obtained from the [[Bazaar]] exploit that allowed players to obtain Bazaar UI items. With this glitch, [[Super Enchanted Egg|Super Enchanted Eggs]] were able to be dropped on the ground and picked up as a vanilla item form.
Spawn Villager eggs can be obtained from killing mobs using weapons with [[Runes|Jerry Rune]] applied above the hoppers, that can pick up the Villager Spawn eggs.
## Impact
While regular Spawn Eggs didn't have any impact on the server, Spawn Villager eggs could be used on [[Jerry]] on [[Private Island|private islands]] to summon baby villagers, that could be traded with when they grew up. This introduced several new unique no-lore items.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Players started obtaining Spawn Egg.|
Linked from: [[Egg (disambiguation)]], [[Glitched Items]], [[Inflatable Jerry]], [[Spawn Villager (NPC)]]

# The Great Potato War
Aliases: Great Potato War, Potato War
Categories: Great Potato War, Events
The Great Potato War was a semi-unofficial event that occurred between users 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid and 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade, who competed to see who could maintain the highest [[Potato]] Collection.
Eventually, it was decided that the first to reach 500 million Potato Collection would win the war.
On June 21, 2020, 8Technoblade reached 500 million Potato Collection, and the [[Potato King]] was added in front of the Map on the [[Hub Island]] for 7 days and gave players exclusive items. It is highly unlikely that the [[NPC]] will be available in the future.
## List of Items related to The War
Some of the related items quote Sun Tzu's "The Art of War", which Technoblade frequently mentioned in his videos.
- [[Potato Talisman]]
  - Given to anyone who talks to the [[Potato King]]
- [[Basket of Hope from the Great Potato War]]
  - Given to those who were ranked top #1000 in Potato Collection
- [[Potato War Silver Medal]]
  - Given to the player ranked #2 in Potato Collection (7im_a_squid_kid)
- [[Potato Crown]]
  - Given to the player ranked #1 in Potato Collection (8Technoblade)
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|The beginning of the Great Potato War.|
|Nov 13, 2019|Technoblade uploaded Skyblock: The Great Potato War.|
|Nov 16, 2019|im a squid kid uploaded The Whitelisted Mystery.|
|Mar 7, 2020|Technoblade uploaded Skyblock: Potato War 2.|
|Mar 7, 2020|im a squid kid uploaded im currently losing the potato war.|
|Apr 1, 2020|im a squid kid uploaded HOW I WON THE GREAT POTATO WAR (APRIL FOOLS).|
|Jun 21, 2020|End of the Great Potato War.|
|Jun 22, 2020|Added [[Potato King]] to the Hub.|
|Jun 22, 2020|Added [[Potato Crown]].|
|Jun 22, 2020|Added [[Potato Talisman]].|
|Jun 22, 2020|Added [[Basket of Hope from the Great Potato War]].|
|Jun 30, 2020|The Potato King is removed from the Hub.|
|Jul 13, 2020|Technoblade uploaded Skyblock: Potato War 3 (FINALE).|
|Jul 20, 2020|im a squid kid uploaded the great potato war is finally over.|
## References
- The first Declaration of the War by Technoblade, taking im_a_squid_kid's #1 Potato Collection
- The second Declaration of the War by Technoblade
- The agreement that whoever reaches 500 million wins the war in Technoblade's Twitter
- The livestream which directly preceded the appearance of the Potato King in Technoblade's Youtube
- Technoblades video of the conclusion

Black Woolen Yarn is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item that can upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Black Cat Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rarity]].
## Obtaining
A Black Woolen Yarn can be purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] in [[Unincorporated]] for 100 [[Dark Candy]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]].
## Usage
Black Woolen Yarn can be given to [[Kat]] as well as 1,000,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Black Cat Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rarity]].
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Black Cat Pet]] unlocks the following perk:
|Perk|Increments|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|-|
|Looting|+0.15%|Gain 0.15% more collection items from monsters!|Gain 15% more collection items from monsters!|

# Boo Stone
Categories: Great Spook Items, Rare items, Items, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Reforge stones
Great Spook Exclusive Boo Stone is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Boo Stone]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOO_STONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Greater Spook reforge when combined with Great Spook armor and equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Fear; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; * Soulbound * RARE REFORGE STONE
The Boo Stone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Greater Spook]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Great Spook Armor]] and Great Spook Equipment. It grants [[Fear]].
## Obtaining
The Boo Stone can be bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 10,000 [[Coins]] after giving them the [[Great Spook Reforging Manual]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Boo Stone can be combined with [[Great Spook Armor]] and its equipment pieces using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Greater Spook]] reforge, to grant [[Fear]], which scales with the item's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 Fr ([[Fear]])|+2 Fr ([[Fear]])|+3 Fr ([[Fear]])|+4 Fr ([[Fear]])|+5 Fr ([[Fear]])|+6 Fr ([[Fear]])|+7 Fr ([[Fear]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Although the reforge applied by this reforge stone is called "Greater Spook", it will replace "Great Spook" with "Greater Spook" in the item name rather than adding the "Greater Spook" prefix to it as other reforges do.

# Commitment Phobia
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Commitment Phobia is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Commitment Phobia]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 1x [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: COMMITMENT_PHOBIA
Commitment Phobia is mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]], after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
Commitment Phobia will walk slowly towards the player once spawned dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. Once they only have 1 [[Health]], they start speaking in chat, alongside their dialogue a message that must be clicked will be sent. After clicking the message Commitment Phobia disappears, giving the player their drops.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Please sign this paper! Click HERE to sign the <document>.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: The spectres of commitment watch over you.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Your echo will resonate in the void.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Your fate is now intertwined with the unseen.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Bound by ink, sealed by destiny.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: You've danced with the shadows; may they be kind to you.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: By your hand, the silent pact is forged.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: May you find solace in the choices you've sealed.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Ink fades, but consequences linger eternally.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Your signature resonates in the silent abyss.|
|After signing the scroll|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: The chains of eternity bind you.|
|Clicking on the outdated message|[FEAR] Commitment Phobia: Trying to pull a fast one? That's not even the paper I gave you!|
## Trivia
- There are various types of documents for the player to sign, such as:
  - Skill Swap Scroll
  - Alliance Pact
  - Debt Bond
  - Eternal Obligation Scroll
  - Pact of the Shadow Realm
  - Soul Pact
  - Quest Contract

|Sep 30, 2026|Changed the [[Shielded]] [[Health]] multiplier in the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) calculation from 1,000x to 100,000x, impacting this mob's level and non-item drops.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Primal Fears mentioned in Patch Notes.|
|Oct 30, 2023|Commitment Phobia can now be spawned.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv100] to [Lv29], [[Coins]] from 0 to 9, [[Experience]] from 0 to 31, and [[Combat]] XP from 6 to 43.|

## Obtaining

Dark Candy is obtained by killing [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]] and the mobs they turn into after killing them.

# Darkness Shade
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Darkness Shade is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Darkness Shade]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 1x [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: DARKNESS_SHADE_100
Darkness Shade is mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook event]], after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
Darkness will blind the player and walk slowly towards them once spawned dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. After being hit, they teleport to a random location near the player and will say up to three lines of dialogue.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]||
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: The darkness consumes all.|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Lost in the void?|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Guess who's behind you...|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: You can't run from the unseen.|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Embrace the night.|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Shadows never lie.|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Closer than you think...|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Where am I?|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Can't see me, can you?|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: Feeling vulnerable?|
|After taking 1 [[Damage]]|[FEAR] Darkness Shade: BOO|
## Bugs
- Occasionally, a Darkness Shade will not die after 5 attacks and requires a 6th hit to kill it.
## Tips
- The player can wear a [[Cow Hat]] to bypass the [[Blindness Potion|Blindness]] effect inflicted by Darkness Shades.

|Sep 30, 2026|Changed the [[Shielded]] [[Health]] multiplier in the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) calculation from 1,000x to 100,000x, impacting this mob's level and non-item drops.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Dead Seed is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Dead Seed can be obtained by using [[Enchanted Bone Meal]] on a [[Dead Bush]], including [[Red Thornleaf|Red Thornleaves]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The [[Enchanted Bone Meal]] will be consumed upon use.
## Usage
[[Dead Seed]] 5 Dead Seeds can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Sword]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].

# Deadline
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Deadline is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Deadline]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 0–1x [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: DEADLINE_100
Deadline is mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook event]], after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
Deadline will walk slowly towards the player once spawned dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. Upon reaching 1 [[Health]], the player is required to stand immobile for 5 seconds before being able to hit Deadline again.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Every heartbeat a silent echo in eternity's corridor.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: In stillness, perceive the dance of fleeting seconds.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: The sands flow, even as you stand still.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Every second a choice, every moment a consequence.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Frozen in the now, yet time marches on.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Awaiting the inevitable tick that follows the tock.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Time is both jailer and liberator.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Endings and beginnings, bound by my rhythm.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Time's embrace tightens with each passing second.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Do you hear the whisper of hours long past?|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Moments lost, never to return.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: You may stop, but I never will.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Stillness won't halt the relentless forward push.|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Deadline: Each pause, a testament to moments gone by.|
## Bugs
- The player must move at least once after the Deadline spawns, otherwise the countdown will get stuck at 5 seconds. It is also possible to move after 99 ticks (4.95s) and still complete the countdown.

The Eerie Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Eerie Pet can be bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 2 [[Ephemeral Gratitude]].
## Upgrading
The Eerie Pet can be upgraded from [[Rarity|COMMON]] to [[Rarity|RARE]] with an [[Eerie Treat]] and from [[Rarity|RARE]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] with an [[Eerie Toy]]. This is accomplished by right clicking the Eerie Pet with the upgrade item while it is equipped. The upgrade item will not be consumed in the process.

[Bonus stats: Speed: +0.1 per level Intelligence: +0.5 per level Abilities: Fearnesy: No leveling bonus Fearama: +0.009090909 damage per Fear per level. Fearcreasing: +0.003 Fear per level.]

## Trivia
- The [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] refers to the Eerie Pet as being a "Great Spook Pet" nicknamed Eerie.
- The Eerie Pet is the first and only pet to skip rarities when upgraded.
- This pet cannot be sold to [[George]]. If a player tries to sell it, it will say "George is not interested in purchasing this pet".
- This pet will have an edition only if purchased during the 2023 event, not for subsequent years.
- The Fearama and Fearcreasing perks used to display their individual editions, but they no longer do so.
- The Eerie pet is not upgraded by Kat, but rather by using its own upgrade items directly on the pet.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Eerie Pet.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Revamped Fearama perk.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Fixed Fearcreasing giving too much [[Fear]].|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Editions of Fearama and Fearcreasing are no longer displayed.|

# Eerie Toy
Categories: Great Spook Items, Epic items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Eerie Toy is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Eerie Toy]
Type: [[Pet Items]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EERIE_TOY
Tooltip: Obtained during the Alchemist experiments!; Turns your RARE Eerie Pet into its LEGENDARY version.; * Soulbound * EPIC PET ITEM
The Eerie Toy is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
The Eerie Toy can be bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 1 [[Nightmare Nullifier]].
## Usage
Right clicking a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Eerie Pet]] with the Eerie Toy while it is equipped will upgrade it to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]. The Eerie Toy will not be consumed in the process.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Eerie Toy.|
|Oct 26, 2023|Eerie Toy becomes obtainable.|
|Oct 26, 2023|Eerie Toy can now be donated to the [[Museum]].|
Linked from: [[Collectable Items]], [[Eerie Pet]], [[Museum]], [[Museum/Items]], [[Museum/Items/Special]], [[Nightmare Nullifier]], [[Pet Upgrades]], [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]

# Eerie Treat
Categories: Great Spook Items, Rare items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Eerie Treat is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Eerie Treat]
Type: [[Pet Items]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EERIE_TREAT
Tooltip: Obtained during the Alchemist experiments!; Turns your COMMON Eerie Pet into its RARE version.; * Soulbound * RARE PET ITEM
The Eerie Treat is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
The Eerie Treat can be bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 10 [[Sewer Fish]].
## Usage
Right clicking a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Eerie Pet]] with the Eerie Treat while it is equipped will upgrade it to [[Rarity|RARE]]. The Eerie Treat will not be consumed in the process.

|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Eerie Treat.|

|Oct 26, 2023|Eerie Treat can now be donated to the [[Museum]].|
